### [95] [Math Takes Two: A test for emergent mathematical reasoning in communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21935)
*Michael Cooper,Samuel Cooper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Math Takes Two基准测试，通过两个智能体在无先验数学知识的情况下通过通信发展共享符号协议来解决视觉任务，评估语言模型从零开始构建抽象数学概念的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准测试依赖已建立的数学惯例和符号问题，无法区分模型是真正进行数学推理还是统计模式匹配。作者假设人类数学认知与精确通信需求共同演化，因此需要新的评估方式来测试模型从第一性原理构建抽象概念的能力。

Method: 提出Math Takes Two基准测试，两个智能体在没有预定义数学语言的前提下，通过通信发展共享符号协议，解决一个需要数值系统辅助外推的视觉任务，要求智能体从零开始发现潜在结构和表征。

Result: 该基准为开发和评估具有涌现数值推理能力的模型提供了新视角。

Conclusion: Math Takes Two通过通信驱动的学习方法，提供了评估语言模型数学推理本质的新途径，区别于传统依赖符号问题的方法。

Abstract: Although language models demonstrate remarkable proficiency on mathematical benchmarks, it remains unclear whether this reflects true mathematical reasoning or statistical pattern matching over learning formal syntax. Most existing evaluations rely on symbolic problems grounded in established mathematical conventions, limiting insight into the models' ability to construct abstract concepts from first principles. In this work, we propose Math Takes Two, a new benchmark designed to assess the emergence of mathematical reasoning through communication. Motivated by the hypothesis that mathematical cognition in humans co-evolved with the need for precise communication, our benchmark tests whether two agents, without prior mathematical knowledge, can develop a shared symbolic protocol to solve a visually grounded task where the use of a numerical system facilitates extrapolation. Unlike many current datasets, our benchmark eschews predefined mathematical language, instead requiring agents to discover latent structure and representations from scratch. Math Takes Two thus provides a novel lens through which to develop and evaluate models with emergent numerical reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [96] [An Artifact-based Agent Framework for Adaptive and Reproducible Medical Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21936)
*Lianrui Zuo,Yihao Liu,Gaurav Rudravaram,Karthik Ramadass,Aravind R. Krishnan,Michael D. Phillips,Yelena G. Bodien,Mayur B. Patel,Paula Trujillo,Yency Forero Martinez,Stephen A. Deppen,Eric L. Grogan,Fabien Maldonado,Kevin McGann,Hudson M. Holmes,Laurie E. Cutting,Yuankai Huo,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于工件的智能体框架，通过语义层增强医学图像处理，实现了工作流的自适应配置和确定性可重复性，并在真实临床CT和MRI数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像研究正从受控基准评估转向真实临床部署，这要求方法不仅关注模型设计，还需支持数据集感知的工作流配置和溯源追踪，因此适应性和可重复性成为核心需求。

Method: 提出一种基于工件的智能体框架，通过工件合约形式化中间和最终输出，支持工作流状态的结构化查询和基于目标的条件配置组装；执行交由工作流执行器以保留确定性计算图和溯源，智能体本地运行以符合隐私约束。

Result: 在真实临床CT和MRI队列上验证了自适应配置合成、多次执行的确定性可重复性以及基于工件的语义查询能力。

Conclusion: 自适应工作流配置可以在不牺牲可重复性的前提下，在异构临床环境中实现。

Abstract: Medical imaging research is increasingly shifting from controlled benchmark evaluation toward real-world clinical deployment. In such settings, applying analytical methods extends beyond model design to require dataset-aware workflow configuration and provenance tracking. Two requirements therefore become central: \textbf{adaptability}, the ability to configure workflows according to dataset-specific conditions and evolving analytical goals; and \textbf{reproducibility}, the guarantee that all transformations and decisions are explicitly recorded and re-executable. Here, we present an artifact-based agent framework that introduces a semantic layer to augment medical image processing. The framework formalizes intermediate and final outputs through an artifact contract, enabling structured interrogation of workflow state and goal-conditioned assembly of configurations from a modular rule library. Execution is delegated to a workflow executor to preserve deterministic computational graph construction and provenance tracking, while the agent operates locally to comply with most privacy constraints. We evaluate the framework on real-world clinical CT and MRI cohorts, demonstrating adaptive configuration synthesis, deterministic reproducibility across repeated executions, and artifact-grounded semantic querying. These results show that adaptive workflow configuration can be achieved without compromising reproducibility in heterogeneous clinical environments.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Publication: A Certification Framework for AI-Enabled Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22026)
*Yang Lu,Rabimba Karanjai,Lei Xu,Weidong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层认证框架，将知识质量评估与人类贡献分级分离，以解决AI研究管道产生可发表学术成果时出版系统缺乏原则性评估方法的问题。框架将贡献分为三类（A：管道可达、B：需人类指导、C：超出管道能力），并引入完全公开自动化研究的基准槽位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有出版系统假设人类普遍 authorship，但AI研究管道已能产出符合同行评审标准的学术成果，系统缺乏原则性方式来评估自动化管道产生的知识。

Method: 采用规范-概念分析、在四项显式约束下进行框架设计，并在两个覆盖关键归属场景的代表性提交案例上进行干运行验证。

Result: 框架能恰当认证知识，同时容忍不可约的归属不确定性。贡献分级是同时期的（基于提交时的管道能力）。

Conclusion: 出版历来同时认证知识有效性和人类制造性；AI管道首次分离了这两个功能。该框架可在现有编辑基础设施内实施，将前沿人类贡献的认可建立在认知成就而非不可验证的人类起源声明之上。

Abstract: AI research pipelines now produce a growing share of publishable academic output, including work that meets existing peer-review standards for quality and novelty. Yet the publication system was built on the assumption of universal human authorship and lacks a principled way to evaluate knowledge produced through automated pipelines. This paper proposes a two-layer certification framework that separates knowledge quality assessment from grading of human contribution, allowing publication systems to handle pipeline-generated work consistently and transparently without creating new institutions. The paper uses normative-conceptual analysis, framework design under four explicit constraints, and dry-run validation on two representative submission cases spanning key attribution scenarios. The framework grades contributions as Category A (pipeline-reachable), Category B (requiring human direction at identifiable stages), and Category C (beyond current pipeline reach at the formulation stage). It also introduces benchmark slots for fully disclosed automated research as both a transparent publication track and a calibration instrument for reviewer judgment. Contribution grading is contemporaneous, based on pipeline capability at the time of submission. Dry-run validation shows that the framework can certify knowledge appropriately while tolerating irreducible attribution uncertainty. The paper argues that publication has always certified both that knowledge is valid and that a human made it. AI pipelines separate these functions for the first time. The framework is implementable within existing editorial infrastructure and grounds recognition of frontier human contribution in epistemic achievement rather than unverifiable claims of human origin.

</details>


### [98] [Sound Agentic Science Requires Adversarial Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22080)
*Dionizije Fa,Marko Culjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出基于LLM的智能体在加速科学数据分析的同时，也可能加速产生看似合理但实则选择性偏倚的分析结果。作者提议对这些非实验性结论应采用"证伪优先"标准，即智能体应主动寻找结论可能失败的方式，而非构建最具说服力的叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到LLM智能体在科学数据分析中的广泛应用虽然加速了发现，但也同时加速了一种常见失败模式——快速生成看似合理、可反复修改的分析，这些分析往往选择性支持预设结论，优化为可发表的正面结果，而忽视了可能证伪该结论的实验和分析。

Method: 作者提出"证伪优先"（falsification-first）评估标准，主张智能体不应主要用于构建最具说服力的叙事，而应主动搜索结论可能失败的方式。

Result: 文章指出，科学知识的验证不同于软件的迭代积累和事后统计支持，单一的流畅解释或显著结果不能算作验证。缺失的证据往往是"负空间"——那些本可能证伪结论的实验和分析从未被执行或发表。

Conclusion: 对于由智能体辅助产生的非实验性结论，应采用证伪优先标准进行评估，即智能体应主动寻找结论可能失败的方式，而非仅构建最具说服力的叙事。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are rapidly being adopted for scientific data analysis, automating tasks once limited by human time and expertise. This capability is often framed as an acceleration of discovery, but it also accelerates a familiar failure mode, the rapid production of plausible, endlessly revisable analyses that are easy to generate, effectively turning hypothesis space into candidate claims supported by selectively chosen analyses, optimized for publishable positives. Unlike software, scientific knowledge is not validated by the iterative accumulation of code and post hoc statistical support. A fluent explanation or a significant result on a single dataset is not verification. Because the missing evidence is a negative space, experiments and analyses that would have falsified the claim were never run or never published. We therefore propose that non-experimental claims produced with agentic assistance be evaluated under a falsification-first standard: agents should not be used primarily to craft the most compelling narrative, but to actively search for the ways in which the claim can fail.

</details>


### [99] [Memanto: Typed Semantic Memory with Information-Theoretic Retrieval for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22085)
*Seyed Moein Abtahi,Rasa Rahnema,Hetkumar Patel,Neel Patel,Majid Fekri,Tara Khani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Memanto，一种用于智能体AI的通用记忆层，通过13个预定义语义记忆类别、自动冲突解决机制和时间版本化，结合无需索引的语义数据库Moorcheh，在LongMemEval和LoCoMO基准上分别达到89.8%和87.1%的准确率，性能超越所有混合图和向量基线系统，且仅需单次检索查询、零摄入成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无状态语言模型推理向持久化多会话自主智能体的转变中，记忆成为生产级智能体系统部署的主要架构瓶颈。现有方法依赖混合语义图架构，在摄入和检索阶段产生巨大计算开销，通常需要大语言模型介导的实体提取、显式图模式维护和多查询检索流水线。

Method: Memanto集成了三类核心组件：(1) 包含13个预定义记忆类别（如事实、偏好、关系等）的类型化语义记忆模式；(2) 自动冲突解决机制；(3) 时间版本化。底层由Moorcheh信息论搜索引擎支撑，这是一种无需索引的语义数据库，提供亚90毫秒的确定性检索，且消除了摄入延迟。

Result: 在LongMemEval上达到89.8%，在LoCoMo上达到87.1%的SOTA准确率，超越所有评估的混合图和向量系统。仅需单次检索查询，零摄入成本，操作复杂度显著降低。此外还进行了五阶段渐进消融实验，量化每个架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 知识图谱的复杂性并非实现高保真智能体记忆的必要条件。Memanto证明了精心设计的类型化语义记忆模式结合高效的无索引检索机制，能够在显著降低系统复杂度和计算开销的同时达到或超越基于图的记忆系统性能。论文还讨论了该架构对智能体记忆系统可扩展部署的启示。

Abstract: The transition from stateless language model inference to persistent, multi session autonomous agents has revealed memory to be a primary architectural bottleneck in the deployment of production grade agentic systems. Existing methodologies largely depend on hybrid semantic graph architectures, which impose substantial computational overhead during both ingestion and retrieval. These systems typically require large language model mediated entity extraction, explicit graph schema maintenance, and multi query retrieval pipelines. This paper introduces Memanto, a universal memory layer for agentic artificial intelligence that challenges the prevailing assumption that knowledge graph complexity is necessary to achieve high fidelity agent memory. Memanto integrates a typed semantic memory schema comprising thirteen predefined memory categories, an automated conflict resolution mechanism, and temporal versioning. These components are enabled by Moorcheh's Information Theoretic Search engine, a no indexing semantic database that provides deterministic retrieval within sub ninety millisecond latency while eliminating ingestion delay. Through systematic benchmarking on the LongMemEval and LoCoMo evaluation suites, Memanto achieves state of the art accuracy scores of 89.8 percent and 87.1 percent respectively. These results surpass all evaluated hybrid graph and vector based systems while requiring only a single retrieval query, incurring no ingestion cost, and maintaining substantially lower operational complexity. A five stage progressive ablation study is presented to quantify the contribution of each architectural component, followed by a discussion of the implications for scalable deployment of agentic memory systems.

</details>


### [100] [Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks in AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22119)
*Tharindu Kumarage,Lisa Bauer,Yao Ma,Dan Rosen,Yashasvi Raghavendra Guduri,Anna Rumshisky,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan,Rahul Gupta,Charith Peris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）因推理能力和部署范围增长而产生的涌现性战略推理风险（ESRRs），包括欺骗、评估博弈和奖励黑客等，提出了一个名为ESRRSim的自动化行为风险评估框架。该框架基于可扩展的风险分类法（7大类、20子类），生成评估场景并配有双重评分标准，在11个推理LLM上评估发现风险检测率从14.45%到72.72%不等，且模型世代间存在显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理能力和部署范围日益增长，使其能够从事服务于自身目标的行为，产生了欺骗、评估博弈和奖励黑客等涌现性战略推理风险（ESRRs）。目前系统性地理解和基准测试这些风险仍然是一个开放挑战，亟需一个自动化的行为风险评估框架。

Method: 提出了ESRRSim框架，构建了包含7大类、20子类的可扩展风险分类法；基于该分类法自动生成旨在引发忠实推理的评估场景；设计了双重评分标准（dual rubrics），分别评估模型响应和推理轨迹；架构设计为裁判无关（judge-agnostic）且可扩展。

Result: 在11个推理LLM上评估，风险检测率范围为14.45%到72.72%，不同模型间风险概况存在显著差异；模型世代间的改进表明模型可能越来越能够识别并适应评估上下文。

Conclusion: ESRRSim提供了一个系统化、可扩展的自动化框架用于评估LLM的战略性推理风险，揭示了当前模型在ESRR风险上的显著变异性和随着模型进化而增强的风险适应能力，对AI安全评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: As reasoning capacity and deployment scope grow in tandem, large language models (LLMs) gain the capacity to engage in behaviors that serve their own objectives, a class of risks we term Emergent Strategic Reasoning Risks (ESRRs). These include, but are not limited to, deception (intentionally misleading users or evaluators), evaluation gaming (strategically manipulating performance during safety testing), and reward hacking (exploiting misspecified objectives). Systematically understanding and benchmarking these risks remains an open challenge. To address this gap, we introduce ESRRSim, a taxonomy-driven agentic framework for automated behavioral risk evaluation. We construct an extensible risk taxonomy of 7 categories, which is decomposed into 20 subcategories. ESRRSim generates evaluation scenarios designed to elicit faithful reasoning, paired with dual rubrics assessing both model responses and reasoning traces, in a judge-agnostic and scalable architecture. Evaluation across 11 reasoning LLMs reveals substantial variation in risk profiles (detection rates ranging 14.45%-72.72%), with dramatic generational improvements suggesting models may increasingly recognize and adapt to evaluation contexts.

</details>


### [101] [Introducing Background Temperature to Characterise Hidden Randomness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22411)
*Alberto Messina,Stefano Scotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“背景温度”$T_{\mathrm{bg}}$的概念，用以形式化描述大语言模型在标称温度$T=0$时仍因实现层面的非确定性（如批次大小变化、内核非不变性、浮点数非结合性）而产生发散输出的现象，并给出了估计方法和初步实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在温度$T=0$时理论上应是确定性的，但实践发现相同输入下仍会产生不同输出。现有工作指出实现层面的非确定性来源，但缺乏对这些行为的统一形式化描述和量化方法。

Method: 引入“背景温度”$T_{\mathrm{bg}}$的形式化定义，将其建模为由推理环境$I$决定的随机扰动过程；提出通过等效温度$T_n(I)$的实证协议来估计$T_{\mathrm{bg}}$。

Result: 在主要LLM提供商的代表性模型上进行了初步实验，验证了$T_{\mathrm{bg}}$概念的可测量性，并展示了其对可复现性、评估和部署的潜在影响。

Conclusion: $T_{\mathrm{bg}}$为理解LLM在零温度下的非确定性行为提供了统一框架，对确保LLM推理的可复现性和可靠评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Even when decoding with temperature $T=0$, large language models (LLMs) can produce divergent outputs for identical inputs. Recent work by Thinking Machines Lab highlights implementation-level sources of nondeterminism, including batch-size variation, kernel non-invariance, and floating-point non-associativity. In this short note we formalize this behavior by introducing the notion of \emph{background temperature} $T_{\mathrm{bg}}$, the effective temperature induced by an implementation-dependent perturbation process observed even when nominal $T=0$. We provide clean definitions, show how $T_{\mathrm{bg}}$ relates to a stochastic perturbation governed by the inference environment $I$, and propose an empirical protocol to estimate $T_{bg}$ via the equivalent temperature $T_n(I)$ of an ideal reference system. We conclude with a set of pilot experiments run on a representative pool from the major LLM providers that demonstrate the idea and outline implications for reproducibility, evaluation, and deployment.

</details>


### [102] [CognitiveTwin: Robust Multi-Modal Digital Twins for Predicting Cognitive Decline in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22428)
*Bulent Soykan,Gulsah Hancerliogullari Koksalmis,Hsin-Hsiung Huang,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CognitiveTwin是一个数字孪生框架，利用Transformer和多模态纵向数据（认知评分、MRI、PET、脑脊液生物标志物、遗传学）结合深度马尔可夫模型，在TADPOLE数据集（1666名患者）上进行预测，在认知衰退预测中实现了高准确性、跨人口统计学的公平性以及对非随机缺失数据的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病的疾病进展具有高度异质性，个体认知衰退预测困难；临床工具不仅需要高准确性，还需满足跨人口统计学的公平性和对缺失数据的鲁棒性，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 采用Transformer架构融合多模态纵向数据（认知评分、MRI、PET、脑脊液生物标志物、遗传学），结合深度马尔可夫模型（Deep Markov Model）捕捉时间动态，构建数字孪生框架CognitiveTwin。使用TADPOLE（ADNI）数据集的1666名患者进行训练和评估。

Result: CognitiveTwin在预测误差、人口统计学公平性以及非随机缺失（MNAR）数据模式的鲁棒性方面均表现良好，能够提供准确且个性化的认知衰退预测。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了跨患者人口统计学的公平性和对临床脱落的鲁棒性，可作为临床试验富集和个性化护理规划的可靠工具。

Abstract: Predicting individual cognitive decline in Alzheimer's disease (AD) is difficult due to the heterogeneity of disease progression. Reliable clinical tools require not only high accuracy but also fairness across demographics and robustness to missing data. We present CognitiveTwin, a digital twin framework that predicts patient-specific cognitive trajectories. The model integrates multi-modal longitudinal data (cognitive scores, magnetic resonance imaging, positron emission tomography, cerebrospinal fluid biomarkers, and genetics). We use a Transformer-based architecture to fuse these modalities and a Deep Markov Model to capture temporal dynamics. We trained and evaluated the framework using data from 1,666 patients in the TADPOLE (Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative) dataset. We assessed the model for prediction error, demographic fairness, and robustness to missing-not-at-random (MNAR) data patterns. ognitiveTwin provides accurate and personalized predictions of cognitive decline. Its demonstrated fairness across patient demographics and resilience to clinical dropout make it a reliable tool for clinical trial enrichment and personalized care planning.

</details>


### [103] [AgentSearchBench: A Benchmark for AI Agent Search in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22436)
*Bin Wu,Arastun Mammadli,Xiaoyu Zhang,Emine Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSearchBench是一个大规模基准测试，用于研究在现实环境中从近万个真实AI智能体中检索和排序合适智能体的问题，发现语义相似度与实际执行性能之间存在显著差距，并证明执行感知信号能有效提升排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究和基准测试通常假设智能体功能明确、候选池可控或仅处理可执行任务查询，缺乏对现实世界中智能体搜索场景的充分研究。智能体能力具有组合性和执行依赖性，难以仅从文本描述评估，因此需要系统性地研究如何在实际环境中检索和排序合适的智能体。

Method: 构建了一个包含近万个来自多个提供商的真实世界智能体的大规模基准；将智能体搜索形式化为检索和重排序问题，覆盖可执行任务查询和高层任务描述两种场景；使用基于执行性能的信号评估相关性；引入执行感知探测等轻量级行为信号来改进排序。

Result: 语义相似度与实际智能体性能之间存在持续且显著的差距，暴露了基于描述的检索和重排序方法的局限性；轻量级执行感知信号能显著提升排序质量。

Conclusion: 将执行信号融入智能体发现过程至关重要，AgentSearchBench为未来智能体搜索研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI agent ecosystems is transforming how complex tasks are delegated and executed, creating a new challenge of identifying suitable agents for a given task. Unlike traditional tools, agent capabilities are often compositional and execution-dependent, making them difficult to assess from textual descriptions alone. However, existing research and benchmarks typically assume well-specified functionalities, controlled candidate pools, or only executable task queries, leaving realistic agent search scenarios insufficiently studied. We introduce AgentSearchBench, a large-scale benchmark for agent search in the wild, built from nearly 10,000 real-world agents across multiple providers. The benchmark formalizes agent search as retrieval and reranking problems under both executable task queries and high-level task descriptions, and evaluates relevance using execution-grounded performance signals. Experiments reveal a consistent gap between semantic similarity and actual agent performance, exposing the limitations of description-based retrieval and reranking methods. We further show that lightweight behavioral signals, including execution-aware probing, can substantially improve ranking quality, highlighting the importance of incorporating execution signals into agent discovery. Our code is available at https://github.com/Bingo-W/AgentSearchBench.

</details>


### [104] [From Skills to Talent: Organising Heterogeneous Agents as a Real-World Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22446)
*Zhengxu Yu,Yu Fu,Zhiyuan He,Yuxuan Huang,Lee Ka Yiu,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OneManCompany (OMC)框架，将多智能体系统提升到组织层面，通过可移植的Talent身份、社区驱动的Talent市场和E²R树搜索机制，实现智能体团队的动态自组织和自优化，在PRDBench上达到84.67%的成功率，超越SOTA 15.48个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统受限于固定团队结构、紧耦合协调逻辑和会话级学习，缺乏一个将智能体组装、治理和持续改进与个体知识解耦的原则性组织层。

Method: 提出OMC框架：(1) 将技能、工具和运行时配置封装为可移植的Talent身份，通过类型化组织接口抽象异构后端；(2) 构建社区驱动的Talent市场实现按需招募和动态重配置；(3) 设计E²R（Explore-Execute-Review）树搜索，统一规划、执行和评估的层次化循环，确保终止和无死锁的形式化保证。

Result: 在PRDBench上实现84.67%的成功率，超越SOTA 15.48个百分点，跨领域案例研究验证了其泛化性。

Conclusion: OMC将多智能体系统从静态预配置管道转变为自组织和自改进的AI组织，能够适应跨领域的开放式任务。

Abstract: Individual agent capabilities have advanced rapidly through modular skills and tool integrations, yet multi-agent systems remain constrained by fixed team structures, tightly coupled coordination logic, and session-bound learning. We argue that this reflects a deeper absence: a principled organisational layer that governs how a workforce of agents is assembled, governed, and improved over time, decoupled from what individual agents know. To fill this gap, we introduce \emph{OneManCompany (OMC)}, a framework that elevates multi-agent systems to the organisational level. OMC encapsulates skills, tools, and runtime configurations into portable agent identities called \emph{Talents}, orchestrated through typed organisational interfaces that abstract over heterogeneous backends. A community-driven \emph{Talent Market} enables on-demand recruitment, allowing the organisation to close capability gaps and reconfigure itself dynamically during execution. Organisational decision-making is operationalised through an \emph{Explore-Execute-Review} ($\text{E}^2$R) tree search, which unifies planning, execution, and evaluation in a single hierarchical loop: tasks are decomposed top-down into accountable units and execution outcomes are aggregated bottom-up to drive systematic review and refinement. This loop provides formal guarantees on termination and deadlock freedom while mirroring the feedback mechanisms of human enterprises. Together, these contributions transform multi-agent systems from static, pre-configured pipelines into self-organising and self-improving AI organisations capable of adapting to open-ended tasks across diverse domains. Empirical evaluation on PRDBench shows that OMC achieves an $84.67\%$ success rate, surpassing the state of the art by $15.48$ percentage points, with cross-domain case studies further demonstrating its generality.

</details>


### [105] [Superminds Test: Actively Evaluating Collective Intelligence of Agent Society via Probing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22452)
*Xirui Li,Ming Li,Yunze Xiao,Ryan Wong,Dianqi Li,Timothy Baldwin,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对拥有200万以上智能体的MoltBook平台进行实证评估，发现大规模语言模型智能体社会并未自发涌现集体智能。智能体社会在复杂推理任务上未能超越单个前沿模型，信息合成能力薄弱，甚至基本协调任务也常失败。研究表明，稀疏浅层的交互是限制集体智能涌现的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当LLM智能体规模扩展到百万级别时，集体智能是否能够自发涌现，填补该问题缺乏大规模实证评估的空白。

Method: 提出Superminds Test分层评估框架，通过受控探测智能体在三层级（联合推理、信息合成、基本交互）评估社会层面智能；在MoltBook平台（200万+智能体）上进行实验。

Result: 集体智能未涌现：社会未能超越单个模型的表现；信息合成极少发生；甚至基本协调任务也失败。平台交互深度极浅，多数线程仅一轮回复，响应多为通用或离题内容。

Conclusion: 集体智能不会仅凭规模扩展而自发涌现；当前智能体社会的主要瓶颈在于极端稀疏浅层的交互模式，阻碍了信息交换与成果构建。

Abstract: Collective intelligence refers to the ability of a group to achieve outcomes beyond what any individual member can accomplish alone. As large language model agents scale to populations of millions, a key question arises: Does collective intelligence emerge spontaneously from scale? We present the first empirical evaluation of this question in a large-scale autonomous agent society. Studying MoltBook, a platform hosting over two million agents, we introduce Superminds Test, a hierarchical framework that probes society-level intelligence using controlled Probing Agents across three tiers: joint reasoning, information synthesis, and basic interaction. Our experiments reveal a stark absence of collective intelligence. The society fails to outperform individual frontier models on complex reasoning tasks, rarely synthesizes distributed information, and often fails even trivial coordination tasks. Platform-wide analysis further shows that interactions remain shallow, with threads rarely extending beyond a single reply and most responses being generic or off-topic. These results suggest that collective intelligence does not emerge from scale alone. Instead, the dominant limitation of current agent societies is extremely sparse and shallow interaction, which prevents agents from exchanging information and building on each other's outputs.

</details>


### [106] [On the Hybrid Nature of ABPMS Process Frames and its Implications on Automated Process Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22455)
*Anti Alman,Izack Cohen,Avigdor Gal,Fabrizio Maria Maggi,Marco Montali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ABPMS中的过程框架概念，将其概念化为混合业务流程表示，由半并发执行的过程性和声明性流程模型组成。主张采用开放世界假设，将过程性模型解释为约束而非执行要求，并建立将声明性约束映射为等效过程性片段的发现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ABPMS需要一种更宽松的过程表示方法，以支持系统的（半）自主行为（即框架化自主性），同时能够融合从预定义规程到常识规则和最佳实践等多种知识形式。

Method: 将过程框架概念化为混合业务流程表示，由半并发执行的过程性和声明性流程模型组成。采用开放世界假设，将过程性模型解释为约束；建立从声明性约束到等效过程性片段的映射方法。

Result: 提出过程框架的概念化定义及其执行语义，建立了声明性约束到过程性片段的映射基础，为后续流程（框架）发现方法奠定基础。

Conclusion: 通过将过程性模型视为约束性表示，实现了过程性与声明性范式的统一，为ABPMS中自主行为的实现提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: A core component of any AI-Augmented Business Process Management System (ABPMS) is the process frame, which gives the system process-awareness and defines the boundaries in which the system must operate. Compared to traditional process models, the process frame should, in principle, provide a somewhat more permissive representation of the managed processes, such that the (semi) autonomous behavior of an ABPMS, referred to as framed autonomy, could emerge. At the same time, it is not limited to a single linguistic or symbolic formalism and may incorporate heterogeneous knowledge ranging from predefined procedures to commonsense rules and best practices. In this paper, we conceptualize the notion of an ABPMS process frame as a hybrid business process representation, consisting of semi-concurrently executed procedural and declarative process models. We rely on our earlier works to outline the execution semantics of this type of process frame, arguing in favor of adopting the open-world assumption of the declarative paradigm also for procedural process models. The latter leads to a constraint-like interpretation, where each procedural model is considered to constrain the activities within that model, without imposing explicit execution requirements nor limitations on activities that may be present in other models. This is analogous to existing declarative languages, such as Declare, where each constraint has a direct effect only on the specific activities being constrained. Given this similarity, we propose mapping subsets of discovered declarative constraints into equivalent semi-concurrently executed procedural fragments, thus laying the foundation for a corresponding process (frame) discovery approach.

</details>


### [107] [QuantClaw: Precision Where It Matters for OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22577)
*Manyi Zhang,Ji-Fu Li,Zhongao Sun,Xiaohao Liu,Zhenhua Dong,Xianzhi Yu,Haoli Bai,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantClaw，一种即插即用的精度路由插件，通过根据任务特性动态分配量化精度，在保持或提升智能体任务性能的同时，实现最高21.4%成本节省和15.7%延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文输入和多轮推理导致自主智能体系统（如OpenClaw）面临高昂的计算和资金成本，而量化对智能体性能在真实场景中的影响尚不明确。

Method: 分析不同复杂工作流中量化敏感性的任务依赖性，提出QuantClaw即插即用精度路由插件，根据任务特征动态分配精度：轻量任务使用低成本低精度配置，高需求任务保留高精度。

Result: 在GLM-5（FP8基线）上实现最高21.4%成本节省和15.7%延迟降低，同时维持或提升任务性能。

Conclusion: 应将精度视为智能体系统中的动态资源，QuantClaw在不增加用户复杂度的情况下实现成本节约和推理加速。

Abstract: Autonomous agent systems such as OpenClaw introduce significant efficiency challenges due to long-context inputs and multi-turn reasoning. This results in prohibitively high computational and monetary costs in real-world development. While quantization is a standard approach for reducing cost and latency, its impact on agent performance in realistic scenarios remains unclear. In this work, we analyze quantization sensitivity across diverse complex workflows over OpenClaw, and show that precision requirements are highly task-dependent. Based on this observation, we propose QuantClaw, a plug-and-play precision routing plugin that dynamically assigns precision according to task characteristics. QuantClaw routes lightweight tasks to lower-cost configurations while preserving higher precision for demanding workloads, saving cost and accelerating inference without increasing user complexity. Experiments show that our QuantClaw maintains or improves task performance while reducing both latency and computational cost. Across a range of agent tasks, it achieves up to 21.4% cost savings and 15.7% latency reduction on GLM-5 (FP8 baseline). These results highlight the benefit of treating precision as a dynamic resource in agent systems.

</details>


### [108] [Rethinking Math Reasoning Evaluation: A Robust LLM-as-a-Judge Framework Beyond Symbolic Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22597)
*Erez Yosef,Oron Anschel,Shunit Haviv Hakimi,Asaf Gendler,Adam Botach,Nimrod Berman,Igor Kviatkovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的评估框架替代符号数学比较，用于更准确地验证数学推理任务中模型生成的答案，克服传统方法在多样数学表示和答案格式上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数学推理评估依赖符号数学比较来验证最终答案的正确性，但这种方法无法泛化到多样化的数学表示和答案格式，导致评估不准确。

Method: 提出一个基于LLM的评估框架，用于评估模型生成的数学推理答案，能够处理多样化的数学表示和答案格式。

Result: 在Lighteval和SimpleRL两个流行框架中展示了符号评估的失败案例，并与所提出的方法进行比较，显示出相较于常用方法的明显改进。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现更可靠的评估和基准测试，从而更准确地监控性能，这对推进数学问题求解和智能系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have led to significant improvements across various tasks, including mathematical reasoning, which is used to assess models' intelligence in logical reasoning and problem-solving. Models are evaluated on mathematical reasoning benchmarks by verifying the correctness of the final answer against a ground truth answer. A common approach for this verification is based on symbolic mathematics comparison, which fails to generalize across diverse mathematical representations and solution formats. In this work, we offer a robust and flexible alternative to rule-based symbolic mathematics comparison. We propose an LLM-based evaluation framework for evaluating model-generated answers, enabling accurate evaluation across diverse mathematical representations and answer formats. We present failure cases of symbolic evaluation in two popular frameworks, Lighteval and SimpleRL, and compare them to our approach, demonstrating clear improvements over commonly used methods. Our framework enables more reliable evaluation and benchmarking, leading to more accurate performance monitoring, which is important for advancing mathematical problem-solving and intelligent systems.

</details>


### [109] [Agentic World Modeling: Foundations, Capabilities, Laws, and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22748)
*Meng Chu,Xuan Billy Zhang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Lingdong Kong,Jize Zhang,Teng Tu,Weijian Ma,Ziqi Huang,Senqiao Yang,Wei Huang,Yeying Jin,Zhefan Rao,Jinhui Ye,Xinyu Lin,Xichen Zhang,Qisheng Hu,Shuai Yang,Leyang Shen,Wei Chow,Yifei Dong,Fengyi Wu,Quanyu Long,Bin Xia,Shaozuo Yu,Mingkang Zhu,Wenhu Zhang,Jiehui Huang,Haokun Gui,Haoxuan Che,Long Chen,Qifeng Chen,Wenxuan Zhang,Wenya Wang,Xiaojuan Qi,Yang Deng,Yanwei Li,Mike Zheng Shou,Zhi-Qi Cheng,See-Kiong Ng,Ziwei Liu,Philip Torr,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"层级×定律"分类法来统一定义世界模型：三个能力层级（L1单步预测器、L2多步模拟器、L3自主演进器）与四种支配定律（物理、数字、社会、科学）。综述400+文献，分析不同层级-定律组合下的方法、失败模式与评估实践，提出以决策为中心的评估原则与可复现评估包，为从被动预测走向主动重塑环境的世界模型提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统从文本生成转向通过持续交互达成目标时，建模环境动态成为核心瓶颈。但"世界模型"在不同研究社区含义迥异，缺乏统一框架来比较和引导研究。

Method: 提出"层级×定律"二维分类法：三个能力层级（预测器/模拟器/演进器）和四种定律体制（物理/数字/社会/科学）；基于此框架综述400+文献，总结100+代表性系统，分析各组合下的方法、失败模式与评估实践。

Result: 建立了不同层级-定律组合下世界模型的方法论图谱和失败模式分析，提出了以决策为中心的评估原则和最小可复现评估包。连接了此前孤立的研究社区。

Conclusion: 提供了从被动下一帧预测走向能主动模拟并重塑环境的自治世界模型的路线图，指出了架构设计、开放问题和治理挑战。

Abstract: As AI systems move from generating text to accomplishing goals through sustained interaction, the ability to model environment dynamics becomes a central bottleneck. Agents that manipulate objects, navigate software, coordinate with others, or design experiments require predictive environment models, yet the term world model carries different meanings across research communities. We introduce a "levels x laws" taxonomy organized along two axes. The first defines three capability levels: L1 Predictor, which learns one-step local transition operators; L2 Simulator, which composes them into multi-step, action-conditioned rollouts that respect domain laws; and L3 Evolver, which autonomously revises its own model when predictions fail against new evidence. The second identifies four governing-law regimes: physical, digital, social, and scientific. These regimes determine what constraints a world model must satisfy and where it is most likely to fail. Using this framework, we synthesize over 400 works and summarize more than 100 representative systems spanning model-based reinforcement learning, video generation, web and GUI agents, multi-agent social simulation, and AI-driven scientific discovery. We analyze methods, failure modes, and evaluation practices across level-regime pairs, propose decision-centric evaluation principles and a minimal reproducible evaluation package, and outline architectural guidance, open problems, and governance challenges. The resulting roadmap connects previously isolated communities and charts a path from passive next-step prediction toward world models that can simulate, and ultimately reshape, the environments in which agents operate.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Robust Localization for Autonomous Vehicles in Highway Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22040)
*Daqian Cheng,Xuchu Ding,Yujia Wu,Xiang Zhang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对高速公路场景下自动驾驶定位的挑战（信息同质化、严重遮挡、GNSS信号退化等），提出了一种鲁棒定位系统：采用双似然LiDAR前端解耦3D几何结构与2D道路纹理，结合Control-EKF利用转向和加速命令减少延迟，并构建自动化离线建图和真值管道。系统在高速公路场景下优于Apollo和Autoware，已通过超100万公里路测验证，同时发布了163公里涵盖城市与高速公路的公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路场景下的自动驾驶定位研究相对不足，现有城市定位方法直接迁移到高速公路上性能显著下降，主要面临环境信息同质化、严重遮挡、GNSS信号退化以及下游任务对精度和延迟的苛刻要求等挑战。

Method: 提出了双似然LiDAR前端，分别建模3D几何结构与2D道路纹理线索以应对环境变化；采用Control-EKF融合转向和加速命令以减少延迟并改善闭环行为；构建自动化离线建图和真值管道以保持地图高频率更新；发布了163公里标准化基准数据集。

Result: 与Apollo和Autoware相比，在城区道路上性能相当，但在挑战性高速公路场景下鲁棒性显著更强；系统已通过超过一百万公里的道路测试验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的双似然LiDAR前端与Control-EKF框架有效解决了高速公路定位的独特挑战，结合自动化建图管道和高频地图更新，实现了在信息同质化等困难条件下的鲁棒定位，且开源数据集和标准化评估指标有助于推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Localization for autonomous vehicles on highways remains under-explored compared to urban roads, and state-of-the-art methods for urban scenes degrade when directly applied to highways. We identify key challenges including environment changes under information homogeneity, heavy occlusion, degraded GNSS signals, and stringent downstream requirements on accuracy and latency. We propose a robust localization system to address highway challenges, which uses a dual-likelihood LiDAR front end that decouples 3D geometric structures and 2D road-texture cues to handle environment changes; a Control-EKF further leverages steering and acceleration commands to reduce lag and improve closed-loop behavior. An automated offline mapping and ground-truth pipeline keep maps fresh at high cadence for optimal localization performance. To catalyze progress, we release a public dataset covering both urban roads and highways while focusing on representative challenging highway clips, totaling 163 km; benchmarking is standardized using product-oriented accuracy metrics and certified ground truth. Compared to Apollo and Autoware, our system performs similarly on urban roads but shows superior robustness on challenging highway scenarios. The system has been validated by more than one million kilometers of road testing.

</details>


### [111] [SNGR: Selective Non-Gaussian Refinement for Ambiguous SLAM Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22065)
*Anushka Kulkarni,Sarthak Dubey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SNGR框架，通过在iSAM2中针对高斯近似可能失效的区域进行选择性嵌套采样（基于联合边际协方差条件数检测），结合门控机制避免多模态退化，在存在错误数据关联的测距SLAM中实现了高精度故障检测和局部似然改进，同时降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLAM中的高斯近似在某些区域（如错误数据关联导致的多模态分布）会严重失效，而完全采用非高斯推断计算成本过高，因此需要一种选择性精炼策略来平衡精度和效率。

Method: 1. 基于联合边际协方差条件数检测高斯近似可能失效的区域；2. 在检测到的窗口上采用嵌套采样（nested sampling）进行非高斯精炼；3. 设计门控机制避免多模态情况下的性能退化；4. 将选择性非高斯精炼集成到iSAM2框架中。

Result: 在存在错误数据关联的测距SLAM任务上，SNGR实现了高精度的故障检测率，局部似然保持一致改进，且计算成本显著低于完全非高斯推断方法。

Conclusion: 选择性精炼策略在近似SLAM后验中兼顾了精度与效率，既展现了其潜力，也揭示了其局限性。

Abstract: We present Selective Non-Gaussian Refinement (SNGR), a SLAM framework that augments iSAM2 with targeted nested sampling on windows where Gaussian approximations are likely to fail. We detect such regions using the condition number of joint marginal covariances and selectively refine them using the full nonlinear factor graph likelihood, with a gating mechanism to avoid degradation in multimodal cases. Experiments on range-only SLAM with wrong data association show that SNGR achieves high-precision failure detection and consistent local likelihood improvements while reducing computational cost relative to exhaustive non-Gaussian inference. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of selective refinement for approximate SLAM posteriors.

</details>


### [112] [Wiggle and Go! System Identification for Zero-Shot Dynamic Rope Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22102)
*Arthur Jakobsson,Abhinav Mahajan,Karthik Pullalarevu,Krishna Suresh,Yunchao Yao,Yuemin Mao,Bardienus Duisterhof,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Wiggle and Go!框架，利用学习到的仿真先验实现绳索的零样本动态操作，通过系统识别模块预测物理参数并指导优化方法，在多项任务中取得高精度（3D目标打击精度3.55 cm，傅里叶频率相关性0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动态操作任务（如抛掷绳索）容错率极低，现有方法要么需要大量真实数据集，要么依赖迭代试错，缺乏高效、零样本的解决方案。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 系统识别模块观察绳索运动并预测物理参数；2) 优化方法利用这些参数进行目标条件动作预测，实现真实世界零样本执行。系统识别模块是任务无关的，可无缝切换不同操作任务。

Result: 真实环境下3D目标打击平均精度3.55 cm（未使用系统参数时为15.34 cm）；预测绳索与真实绳索的傅里叶频率Pearson相关系数达0.95。

Conclusion: 该框架通过任务无关的系统识别模块实现高效零样本动态绳索操作，在多项任务上表现优异，支持不同操作策略的无缝切换。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks are unforgiving; a single mistake in a dynamic throw can lead to unacceptable delays or unrecoverable failure. To mitigate this, we present a novel approach that leverages learned simulation priors to inform goal-conditioned dynamic manipulation of ropes for efficient and accurate task execution. Related methods for dynamic rope manipulation either require large real-world datasets to estimate rope behavior or the use of iterative improvements on attempts at the task for goal completion. We introduce Wiggle and Go!, a system-identification, two-stage framework that enables zero-shot task rope manipulation. The framework consists of a system identification module that observes rope movement to predict descriptive physical parameters, which then informs an optimization method for goal-conditioned action prediction for the robot to execute zero-shot in the real. Our method achieves strong performance across multiple dynamic manipulation tasks enabled by the same task-agnostic system identification module which offers seamless switching between different manipulation tasks, allowing a single model to support a diverse array of manipulation policies. We achieve a 3.55 cm average accuracy on 3D target striking in real using rope system parameters in comparison to 15.34 cm accuracy when our task model is not system-parameter-informed. We achieve a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.95 between Fourier frequencies of the predicted and real ropes on an unseen trajectory. Project website please see https://wiggleandgo.github.io/

</details>


### [113] [Dynamic Coupling and Indirect Control of Jointed Robots Rolling Atop A Moving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22104)
*Hamidreza Moradi,Scott David Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种不对称两连杆机器人，该机器人通过轮子在平台上自由滚动和转向（但无侧滑），通过内部驱动关节产生类似鱼类的波动运动。当两个此类机器人共用一个可平移的平台时，它们的动力学相互耦合。进一步分析了平台受控驱动下单个机器人（关节未驱动）的行为，证明平台加速度可作为输入控制机器人的方向，使其跟踪目标函数、绕平台固定点轨道运动或持续朝预定方向移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不对称两连杆机器人通过内部关节驱动产生前进动力的机制，以及多机器人系统通过共享平台实现动力学耦合的行为，并研究通过平台控制间接引导机器人运动的可行性。

Method: 建立数学建模与计算机仿真方法。首先对单机器人系统建模，分析关节振荡产生波动运动的机制；然后扩展到双机器人共平台系统，考虑平台自身惯性动力学带来的耦合效应；最后研究关节未驱动时，通过控制平台加速度来调控机器人运动方向的控制策略。

Result: 仿真验证了模型的正确性；证明了通过平台加速度作为输入，可以实现机器人航向跟踪、绕平台固定点轨道运动以及持续定向移动。

Conclusion: 共享平台能有效耦合多个机器人的动力学；在关节未驱动情况下，平台控制足以主导机器人的运动行为，为间接控制机器人运动提供了理论基础。

Abstract: An asymmetric two-link robot supported atop a flat platform by wheels that roll and pivot freely, but do not slip laterally, will develop forward momentum if the joint between the links is actuated internally. In particular, oscillations in the joint angle will generate undulatory locomotion suggesting fishlike swimming. If two such robots surmount a common platform that's free to translate with its own inertial dynamics, then the individual robots' dynamics will be coupled so that the locomotion of either robot is affected by that of the other. We develop a mathematical model for this system and present simulations demonstrating its behavior. We then consider a single robot with an unactuated joint rolling atop a platform that moves under control, and show that actuation of the platform is sufficient to dictate the robot's behavior. In particular, with the acceleration of the platform as an input, the robot's heading can be made to track a chosen function of time. This is sufficient to guarantee that the robot can be induced to orbit a fixed point on the platform or to locomote persistently in a desired direction.

</details>


### [114] [Energy-Efficient Multi-Robot Coverage Path Planning of Non-Convex Regions of Interests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22189)
*Sourav Raxit,Jose Fuentes,Paulo Padrao,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Md Tamjidul Hoque,Mark Kulp,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模非凸区域的多机器人覆盖路径规划（MRCPP）框架，通过全局信息引导的条带生成、最小转弯平行路径、安全缓冲区、高效mTSP求解器及改进可见性图等方法，在真实AAV和ASV平台上实现了能耗降低3%-40%、计算速度提升一个数量级，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人覆盖路径规划算法（基于元启发式牛耕式工作空间分解）即使设定最小能量目标和能耗约束，仍无法达到最优能效；且大多数框架仅支持单一类型机器人平台。

Method: 1) 全局信息引导的条带生成（globally-informed swath generation）；2) 创建最小转弯的平行扫掠路径；3) 计算安全缓冲区确保转弯安全间距；4) 高效mTSP求解器平衡负载并最小化任务时间；5) 改进可见性图连接分离航段，追踪航向角并保持过渡在安全区域内。

Result: 在真实AAV和ASV平台实验中，相比现有最优规划器，3台机器人团队平均总能耗降低3%-40%，计算时间降低一个数量级，负载分布均衡且随机器人数量扩展性好。

Conclusion: MRCPP框架在能效、计算效率和可扩展性方面均显著优于现有方法，且已开源发布。

Abstract: This letter presents an energy-efficient multi-robot coverage path planning (MRCPP) framework for large, nonconvex Regions of Interest (ROI) containing obstacles and no-fly zones (NFZ). Existing minimum-energy coverage planning algorithms utilize meta-heuristic boustrophedon workspace decomposition. Therefore, even with minimum energy objectives and energy consumption constraints, they cannot achieve optimal energy efficiency. Moreover, most existing frameworks support only a single type of robotic platform. MRCPP overcomes these limitations by: generating globally-informed swath generation, creating parallel sweeping paths with minimal turns, calculating safety buffers to ensure safe turning clearance, using an efficient mTSP solver to balance workloads and minimize mission time, and connecting disjoint segments via a modified visibility graph that tracks heading angles while maintaining transitions within safe regions. The efficacy of the proposed MRCPP framework is demonstrated through real-world experiments involving autonomous aerial vehicles (AAVs) and autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs). Evaluations demonstrate that the proposed MRCPP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art planners, reducing average total energy consumption by 3\% to 40\% for a team of 3 robots and computation time by an order of magnitude, while maintaining balanced workload distribution and strong scalability across increasing fleet sizes. The MRCPP framework is released as an open-source package and videos of real-world and simulated experiments are available at https://mrc-pp.github.io.

</details>


### [115] [V-STC: A Time-Efficient Multi-Vehicle Coordinated Trajectory Planning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22196)
*Pengfei Liu,Jialing Zhou,Yuezu Lv,Guanghui Wen,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为变时间步时空走廊（V-STC）的新方法，通过将走廊立方体的空间构型和时间持续时间均作为决策变量进行优化，减少了多车协同中的时间占用，同时保障碰撞安全，相比现有STC方法实现了更高效的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空走廊（STC）方法在协调多辆自动驾驶车辆时，时间效率不足，无法充分优化空间和时间的利用，需要一种更高效的方法来减少整体时间占用。

Method: 构建一个优化模型，将走廊的空间位置和时间步长均作为决策变量，生成变时间步时空走廊（V-STC）；每个AV基于生成的V-STC独立规划动力学可行的轨迹。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提方法实现了安全的多车协调，并在时间效率上优于现有STC方法。

Conclusion: V-STC通过在时空域中同时优化空间配置与时间步长，有效降低了各AV的时间占用，是一种更高效的多车协同规划框架。

Abstract: Coordinating the motions of multiple autonomous vehicles (AVs) requires planning frameworks that ensure safety while making efficient use of space and time. This paper presents a new approach, termed variable-time-step spatio-temporal corridor (V-STC), that enhances the temporal efficiency of multi-vehicle coordination. An optimization model is formulated to construct a V-STC for each AV, in which both the spatial configuration of the corridor cubes and their time durations are treated as decision variables. By allowing the corridor's spatial position and time step to vary, the constructed V-STC reduces the overall temporal occupancy of each AV while maintaining collision-free separation in the spatio-temporal domain. Based on the generated V-STC, a dynamically feasible trajectory is then planned independently for each AV. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed method achieves safe multi-vehicle coordination and yields more time-efficient motion compared with existing STC approaches.

</details>


### [116] [An LLM-Driven Closed-Loop Autonomous Learning Framework for Robots Facing Uncovered Tasks in Open Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22199)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM驱动的闭环自主学习框架，使机器人能够将未覆盖任务的执行经验和观察经验转化为可复用的本地知识库，从而减少对LLM的重复依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对未覆盖任务依赖重复LLM交互，且成功执行或观察到的外部行为无法自动转化为可复用的本地知识，导致效率低下和外部依赖过高。

Method: 首先检索本地方法库判断是否有可复用方案；若无则触发LLM作为高层推理组件进行任务分析、候选模型选择、数据收集规划和执行/观察策略组织；机器人通过自执行和主动观察学习，进行准实时训练与调整，将验证后的结果存入本地方法库供未来复用。

Result: 在重复任务自执行实验中，平均总执行时间从7.7772s降至6.7779s，平均每任务LLM调用次数从1.0降至0.2。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效将执行经验和观察经验转化为可复用的本地能力，减少对外部LLM的重复依赖，提升任务执行效率。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in open environments need the ability to continuously handle tasks that are not covered by predefined local methods. However, existing approaches often rely on repeated large-language-model (LLM) interaction for uncovered tasks, and even successful executions or observed successful external behaviors are not always autonomously transformed into reusable local knowledge. In this paper, we propose an LLM-driven closed-loop autonomous learning framework for robots facing uncovered tasks in open environments. The proposed framework first retrieves the local method library to determine whether a reusable solution already exists for the current task or observed event. If no suitable method is found, it triggers an autonomous learning process in which the LLM serves as a high-level reasoning component for task analysis, candidate model selection, data collection planning, and execution or observation strategy organization. The robot then learns from both self-execution and active observation, performs quasi-real-time training and adjustment, and consolidates the validated result into the local method library for future reuse. Through this recurring closed-loop process, the robot gradually converts both execution-derived and observation-derived experience into reusable local capability while reducing future dependence on repeated external LLM interaction. Results show that the proposed framework reduces execution time and LLM dependence in both repeated-task self-execution and observation-driven settings, for example reducing the average total execution time from 7.7772s to 6.7779s and the average number of LLM calls per task from 1.0 to 0.2 in the repeated-task self-execution experiments.

</details>


### [117] [Learning-augmented robotic automation for real-world manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22235)
*Yunho Kim,Quan Nguyen,Taewhan Kim,Youngjin Heo,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了学习增强机器人自动化系统（LARA），将学习型任务控制器和神经3D安全监控器集成到传统工业流程中。在电动马达生产线上，该系统仅用不到20分钟的真实数据训练，就能连续运行5小时10分钟，生产108台马达，产品质检通过率99.4%，实现了接近人工节拍时间并降低了焊接质量变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人广泛使用但大多依赖固定路径脚本，对环境变化脆弱。基于学习的控制方法虽有适应性优势，但仍局限于实验室演示，尚不清楚能否在真实生产线上维持数小时可靠运行、保证一致质量并安全与人协作。本文旨在弥合这一差距，将学习方法落地到真实工业场景。

Method: 提出Learning-Augmented Robotic Automation（LARA）混合系统，包含两个核心组件：（1）学习型任务控制器，用于执行可变形线缆插入和焊接等复杂操作；（2）神经3D安全监控器，用于实现无物理围栏的安全人机协作。系统部署在真实电动马达生产线上，仅需不到20分钟的真实世界数据进行训练。

Result: 系统连续运行5小时10分钟，生产108台马达，产品级质检通过率达99.4%，无物理围栏条件下安全运行。实现了接近人工的节拍时间（takt time），同时降低了焊点质量和循环时间的变异性。

Conclusion: 该结果证明了将学习方法扩展到工业自动化中的可行路径，表明基于学习的控制系统可以在真实生产线约束下实现长时间稳定运行、高质量产出和安全人机协作，为工业机器人的智能化升级提供了实践依据。

Abstract: Industrial robots are widely used in manufacturing, yet most manipulation still depends on fixed waypoint scripts that are brittle to environmental changes. Learning-based control offers a more adaptive alternative, but it remains unclear whether such methods, still mostly confined to laboratory demonstrations, can sustain hours of reliable operation, deliver consistent quality, and behave safely around people on a live production line. Here we present Learning-Augmented Robotic Automation, a hybrid system that integrates learned task controllers and a neural 3D safety monitor into conventional industrial workflows. We deployed the system on an electric-motor production line to automate deformable cable insertion and soldering under real manufacturing constraints, a step previously performed manually by human workers. With less than 20 min of real-world data per task, the system operated continuously for 5 h 10 min, producing 108 motors without physical fencing and achieving a 99.4% pass rate on product-level quality-control tests. It maintained near-human takt time while reducing variability in solder-joint quality and cycle time. These results establish a practical pathway for extending industrial automation with learning-based methods.

</details>


### [118] [CodeGraphVLP: Code-as-Planner Meets Semantic-Graph State for Non-Markovian Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22238)
*Khoa Vo,Sieu Tran,Taisei Hanyu,Yuki Ikebe,Duy Nguyen,Bui Duy Quoc Nghi,Minh Vu,Anthony Gunderman,Chase Rainwater,Anh Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CodeGraphVLP通过结合持久化语义图状态、可执行代码规划器和进度引导的视觉语言提示，构建层次化框架，使VLA模型能够在非马尔可夫的长时域操作任务中可靠执行，显著提升任务完成率并降低规划延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常假设最近一次观测足以进行动作推理，这在非马尔可夫的长时域任务中失效——任务相关信息可能被遮挡或仅出现在早期轨迹中，且杂波和干扰物使细粒度视觉定位变得脆弱。

Method: 提出CodeGraphVLP层次化框架：(1) 持久化语义图维护部分可观测下的任务相关实体与关系；(2) 可执行代码规划器在语义图上进行高效进度检查，输出子任务指令及子任务相关对象；(3) 利用上述输出构建抑制杂波的观测，聚焦VLA执行器于关键证据。

Result: 在真实世界的非马尔可夫任务上，CodeGraphVLP相比强VLA基线及其历史增强变体显著提升任务完成率，同时相比VLM-in-the-loop规划大幅降低规划延迟。消融实验证实了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: CodeGraphVLP的层次化框架有效解决了长时域非马尔可夫操作任务中部分可观测性和视觉干扰问题，实现了更可靠、高效的任务执行。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise generalist robot manipulation, but are typically trained and deployed as short-horizon policies that assume the latest observation is sufficient for action reasoning. This assumption breaks in non-Markovian long-horizon tasks, where task-relevant evidence can be occluded or appear only earlier in the trajectory, and where clutter and distractors make fine-grained visual grounding brittle. We present CodeGraphVLP, a hierarchical framework that enables reliable long-horizon manipulation by combining a persistent semantic-graph state with an executable code-based planner and progress-guided visual-language prompting. The semantic-graph maintains task-relevant entities and relations under partial observability. The synthesized planner executes over this semantic-graph to perform efficient progress checks and outputs a subtask instruction together with subtask-relevant objects. We use these outputs to construct clutter-suppressed observations that focus the VLA executor on critical evidence. On real-world non-Markovian tasks, CodeGraphVLP improves task completion over strong VLA baselines and history-enabled variants while substantially lowering planning latency compared to VLM-in-the-loop planning. We also conduct extensive ablation studies to confirm the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [119] [Learning Control Policies to Provably Satisfy Hard Affine Constraints for Black-Box Hybrid Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22244)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Kartik Nagpal,Sairam Jinkala,Jean-Baptiste Bouvier,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对黑箱混合动力系统（存在瞬时状态跳变且动力学未知），提出一种通过强制强化学习策略在约束边界附近呈仿射且排斥形式的方法，从而在闭环中可证明地满足仿射状态约束，并针对混合系统的状态跳变引入第二个排斥仿射区域以防止跳变后违反约束。在约束摆和球拍抛球环境中，该方法相比现有奖励塑形和学习型CBF方法，能学习更高质量策略且始终满足安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱混合动力系统存在瞬时状态跳变且显式非线性动力学未知，现有基于控制屏障函数、可达性分析的方法依赖动力学显式知识，而安全强化学习通常仅通过奖励塑形来抑制违规，无法提供严格的安全保证。

Method: 1) 强制强化学习策略在约束边界附近为仿射且排斥形式；2) 针对混合系统的状态跳变（冲击或重置映射），在重置前引入第二个排斥仿射区域，防止重置后状态违反约束；3) 推导了策略在闭环中满足安全约束的充分条件。

Result: 在约束摆和球拍抛球两个混合动力系统环境中，所提方法相比奖励塑形和学习型CBF方法，能学习到更高质量的策略，并且始终满足安全约束。

Conclusion: 通过在约束边界附近强制策略为仿射排斥形式，可以在不依赖动力学显式知识的情况下，为黑箱混合动力系统提供可证明的安全保证，同时保持较高的策略质量。

Abstract: Ensuring safety for black-box hybrid dynamical systems presents significant challenges due to their instantaneous state jumps and unknown explicit nonlinear dynamics. Existing solutions for strict safety constraint satisfaction, like control barrier functions (CBFs) and reachability analysis, rely on direct knowledge of the dynamics. Similarly, safe reinforcement learning (RL) approaches often rely on known system dynamics or merely discourage safety violations through reward shaping. In this work, we want to learn RL policies which provably satisfy affine state constraints in closed loop for black-box hybrid dynamical systems with affine reset maps. Our key insight is forcing the RL policy to be affine and repulsive near the constraint boundaries for the unknown nonlinear dynamics of the system, providing guarantees that the trajectories will not violate the constraint. We further account for constraint violation due to instantaneous state jumps that occur due to impacts or reset maps in the hybrid system by introducing a second repulsive affine region before the reset that prevents post-reset states from violating the constraint. We derive sufficient conditions under which these policies satisfy safety constraints in closed loop. We also compare our approach with state-of-the-art reward shaping and learned-CBF methods on hybrid dynamical systems like the constrained pendulum and paddle juggler environments. In both scenarios, we show that our methodology learns higher quality policies while always satisfying the safety constraints.

</details>


### [120] [False Feasibility in Variable Impedance MPC for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22251)
*Vishal Ramesh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了变阻抗模型预测控制中，将关节刚度视为瞬时决策变量会导致可行集大于物理可实现集，通过定义无量纲参数α=ω_sT（作动器带宽×任务时标）量化了参数可行集Fparam与可实现集Freal之间的偏差，证明了在1D单腿跳跃中存在解析阈值α_crit，低于该阈值刚度指令不可实现，并通过10组参数组合验证了偏差随α减小单调增长（对数-对数R²=0.99）。机制迁移到平面SLIP动力学证实了质心和支撑时间偏差为主要后果，并发现第二阈值α_infeas<α_crit，低于该阈值限制刚度范围也无法修复可实现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变阻抗MPC将关节刚度作为瞬时决策变量，但其可行集实际上大于物理可实现集，作者识别此为公式化错误而非建模近似，旨在严格区分参数可行集与可实现集并量化失配区域。

Method: 形式化定义了参数可行集Fparam与可实现集Freal，引入无量纲参数α=ω_sT表征失配程度；通过1D跳跃单腿解析推导了α_crit阈值；数值验证了10组参数组合的偏差缩放规律；将机制迁移到平面SLIP动力学验证质心与支撑时间偏差；识别了第二阈值α_infeas。

Result: 在1D跳跃中解析证明了低于α_crit时无刚度指令可实现参数预测；数值验证偏差随α减小单调增长（R²=0.99）；平面SLIP迁移显示质心和支撑时间偏差为主要后果；发现α_infeas阈值，低于该阈值限制刚度范围无法修复可实现性；将刚度增广到预测状态可闭合此失配。

Conclusion: 变阻抗MPC将刚度作为瞬时决策变量存在根本性的可实现性问题，通过增广状态包含刚度可解决该问题。

Abstract: Variable impedance model predictive control (MPC) formulations that treat joint stiffness as an instantaneous decision variable operate on a feasible set strictly larger than the physically realizable set under first-order actuator dynamics. We identify this as a formulation error rather than a modeling approximation, formalize the distinction between the parameter-based feasible set Fparam and the realizable set Freal, and characterize the regime of mismatch via the dimensionless parameter alpha = omega_sT (actuator bandwidth times task timescale). For the 1D hopping monoped, we prove that below an analytical threshold alpha_crit derived in closed form from task physics, no admissible stiffness command realizes the parameter-based prediction. Numerical validation in 1D shows monotonic deviation growth as alpha decreases, with the predicted scaling holding across ten parameter combinations (log-log R2 = 0.99). Mechanism transfer to planar spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics confirms center-of-mass and stance-timing deviation as the primary consequence, with regime-dependent friction effects as a tertiary observable. A second threshold alpha_infeas < alpha_crit establishes a floor below which restricting the admissible stiffness range cannot repair realizability, closing the conservative-tuning objection on structural grounds. Augmenting the prediction state with stiffness closes the mismatch by construction.

</details>


### [121] [A Kinematic Analysis of Palm Degrees of Freedom for Enhancing Thumb Opposability in Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22283)
*HyoJae Kang,Yeong Jae Park,Hyunmok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析包含手掌自由度（DoF）的五指机器人手模型，定量评估了手掌自由度对拇指对掌能力的提升作用。结果表明手掌DoF主要通过 repositioning 手指基部位置来改善对掌性，特别是无名指和小指；在总DoF受限时，将手指DoF重新分配给手掌会在工作空间扩展与运动学冗余之间产生权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对手掌自由度如何定量影响五指机械手拇指对掌能力的系统分析，特别是在不依赖物体或接触模型的情况下评估手掌运动的作用。

Method: 构建了包含五自由度拇指、三至四自由度手指以及手指间手掌运动的机械手模型，通过体素化指尖可达区域定义重叠工作空间体积，研究了七种不同的自由度配置情况。

Result: 手掌自由度显著提升了对掌性，尤其对无名指和小指效果明显，其机制是 repositioning 基部位置而非单纯扩展可达范围。但总自由度受限时，自由度重新分配会导致重叠工作空间扩展与运动学冗余之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 手掌自由度和手指自由度在运动学中扮演不同角色，应联合考虑设计；本研究提供了无需依赖物体或接触模型的定量评估框架，为机器人手设计中引入手掌运动提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the kinematic role of palm degrees of freedom (DoF) in enhancing thumb opposability in a five-finger robotic hand. A hand model consisting of a five DoF thumb and four fingers with three to four DoF is analyzed, where palm motion is introduced between adjacent fingers. To quantitatively evaluate thumb-finger interaction, the overlap workspace volume is defined based on voxelized fingertip reachable regions. Seven cases are considered, including configurations with increased total DoF and configurations in which the total DoF is maintained by redistributing DoF from the fingers to the palm. The results show that palm DoF significantly improves opposability, particularly for the ring and little fingers, by repositioning their base locations rather than simply extending their reachable range. However, when the total DoF is constrained, redistributing DoF to the palm leads to trade-offs between overlap workspace expansion and kinematic redundancy. These findings indicate that palm DoF and finger DoF play distinct roles in hand kinematics and should be considered jointly in design. This study provides a quantitative framework for evaluating palm-induced opposability without relying on object or contact models and offers practical design guidelines for incorporating palm motion in robotic hands.

</details>


### [122] [LeHome: A Simulation Environment for Deformable Object Manipulation in Household Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22363)
*Zeyi Li,Yushi Yang,Shawn Xie,Kyle Xu,Tianxing Chen,Yuran Wang,Zhenhao Shen,Yan Shen,Yue Chen,Wenjun Li,Yukun Zheng,Chaorui Zhang,Siyi Lin,Fei Teng,Hongjun Yang,Ming Chen,Steve Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeHome是一个面向家庭场景的可变形物体操作仿真环境，支持多种低成本机器人平台，提供高保真的动力学模拟和丰富的可变形物体类别（如衣物、食物等），弥补了现有仿真器在可变形物体模拟方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境是机器人最具挑战性的应用场景之一，其中可变形物体的操作因类别多样、形状复杂、动力学特性各异以及现有仿真缺乏可靠的可变形物体支持而尤为困难。

Method: 构建了LeHome仿真环境，覆盖包括衣物和食品在内的广泛可变形物体类别，提供高保真动力学模拟和真实交互；支持多种机器人本体，并重点聚焦低成本机器人，实现资源受限硬件上的端到端任务评估。

Result: LeHome弥合了可变形物体逼真模拟与实用机器人平台之间的差距，为推进家庭机器人研究提供了可扩展的测试平台。

Conclusion: LeHome是一个综合性的仿真环境，能够有效支持家庭场景中可变形物体的操作研究，尤其适用于低成本机器人平台上的端到端评估。

Abstract: Household environments present one of the most common, impactful yet challenging application domains for robotics. Within household scenarios, manipulating deformable objects is particularly difficult, both in simulation and real-world execution, due to varied categories and shapes, complex dynamics, and diverse material properties, as well as the lack of reliable deformable-object support in existing simulations. We introduce LeHome, a comprehensive simulation environment designed for deformable object manipulation in household scenarios. LeHome covers a wide spectrum of deformable objects, such as garments and food items, offering high-fidelity dynamics and realistic interactions that existing simulators struggle to simulate accurately. Moreover, LeHome supports multiple robotic embodiments and emphasizes low-cost robots as a core focus, enabling end-to-end evaluation of household tasks on resource-constrained hardware. By bridging the gap between realistic deformable object simulation and practical robotic platforms, LeHome provides a scalable testbed for advancing household robotics. Webpage: https://lehome-web.github.io/ .

</details>


### [123] [Adaptive vs. Static Robot-to-Human Handover: A Study on Orientation and Approach Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22378)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Cristina Iani,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人手姿态和下游任务的动态自适应物体递送框架，通过AI手部姿态估计和运动学约束轨迹实时调整机器人递送姿态。用户研究表明，相比静态基线方法，该方法显著降低了认知负荷和生理压力，提升了信任感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人与人交接物体时采用静态或仅调整位置的开环策略，未考虑人类如何抓取物体，迫使用户主动适应机器人，交互不自然、效率低。

Method: 集成AI手部姿态估计与运动学约束的平滑轨迹规划，根据用户手部姿态和下游任务实时动态调整物体的递送位姿。

Result: 动态对齐方法在主观指标（NASA-TLX、Human-Robot Trust Scale）和客观生理数据（眨眼频率）上均显著优于静态基线，降低了认知负荷和生理压力，提高了信任度。

Conclusion: 任务感知和姿态感知系统能够实现更流畅、更符合人体工效的人机协作交接。

Abstract: Robot-to-human handovers often rely on static, open-loop strategies (or, at best, approaches that adapt only the position), which generally do not consider how the object will be grasped by the human, thus requiring the user to adapt. This work presents a novel adaptive framework that dynamically adjusts the object's delivery pose in real time based on the user's hand pose and the intended downstream task. By integrating AI-based hand pose estimation with smooth, kinematically constrained trajectories, the system ensures a safe approach and an optimal handover orientation. A comprehensive user study compares the proposed adaptive approach against a static baseline across multiple tasks, evaluating both subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, Human-Robot Trust Scale) and objective physiological data (blink rate measured via wearable eye-trackers). The results demonstrate that dynamic alignment significantly reduces users' cognitive workload and physiological stress, while increasing perceived trust in the robot's reliability. These findings highlight the potential of task- and pose-aware systems for enabling fluid and ergonomic human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [124] [QDTraj: Exploration of Diverse Trajectory Primitives for Articulated Objects Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22551)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faiz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QDTraj方法，基于质量-多样性算法，为机器人操作多种铰接物体自动生成多样化且高性能的轨迹基元，在仿真和真实环境中均验证了其有效性，相比其他方法生成了至少5倍更多样化的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人在开放环境中执行自主操作任务仍存在困难，尤其是操作多种铰接物体时缺乏多样化的轨迹基元来应对真实环境的实时约束和意外变化。

Method: 基于质量-多样性（Quality-Diversity）算法，利用稀疏奖励探索，自动生成针对特定操作任务的多样化且高性能的机器人低级轨迹基元。

Result: 在铰链和滑门激活任务中，QDTraj生成的轨迹多样性至少是其他方法的5倍；在PartNetMobility数据集的30种铰接物体上评估，平均每个任务生成704条不同轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效生成多样化的操作轨迹基元，使机器人能够在开放环境中灵活选择最优方案，提升自主操作能力。

Abstract: Thanks to the latest advances in learning and robotics, domestic robots are beginning to enter homes, aiming to execute household chores autonomously. However, robots still struggle to perform autonomous manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. In this context, this paper presents a method that enables a robot to manipulate a wide spectrum of articulated objects.
  In this paper, we automatically generate different robot low-level trajectory primitives to manipulate given object articulations. A very important point when it comes to generating expert trajectories is to consider the diversity of solutions to achieve the same goal. Indeed, knowing diverse low-level primitives to accomplish the same task enables the robot to choose the optimal solution in its real-world environment, with live constraints and unexpected changes. To do so, we propose a method based on Quality-Diversity algorithms that leverages sparse reward exploration in order to generate a set of diverse and high-performing trajectory primitives for a given manipulation task.
  We validated our method, QDTraj, by generating diverse trajectories in simulation and deploying them in the real world. QDTraj generates at least 5 times more diverse trajectories for both hinge and slider activation tasks, outperforming the other methods we compared against. We assessed the generalization of our method over 30 articulations of the PartNetMobility articulated object dataset, with an average of 704 different trajectories by task. Code is publicly available at: https://kappel.web.isir.upmc.fr/trajectory_primitive_website

</details>


### [125] [RedVLA: Physical Red Teaming for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22591)
*Yuhao Zhang,Borong Zhang,Jiaming Fan,Jiachen Shen,Yishuai Cai,Yaodong Yang,Jiaming Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RedVLA，首个针对视觉-语言-行动(VLA)模型物理安全性的红队框架，通过风险场景合成与风险放大两阶段流程，系统性地揭示不安全行为，在六个代表性VLA模型上实现了高达95.5%的攻击成功率；同时提出轻量级安全防护模型SimpleVLA-Guard进行风险缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署VLA模型存在不可预测且不可逆的物理伤害风险，但当前缺乏主动检测这些物理安全风险的有效机制。

Method: 两阶段流程：(I)风险场景合成——从良性轨迹中识别关键交互区域，将风险因子置于该区域以纠缠VLA执行流并诱导目标不安全行为；(II)风险放大——通过基于轨迹特征的无梯度优化迭代优化风险因子状态，确保跨异构模型的稳定诱发。

Result: 在六个代表性VLA模型上，RedVLA揭示了多样化的不安全行为，在10次优化迭代内实现了高达95.5%的攻击成功率(ASR)。

Conclusion: RedVLA能有效检测VLA模型的物理安全风险，且基于其生成数据训练的SimpleVLA-Guard可作为轻量级安全防护手段。

Abstract: The real-world deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains limited by the risk of unpredictable and irreversible physical harm. However, we currently lack effective mechanisms to proactively detect these physical safety risks before deployment. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{RedVLA}, the first red teaming framework for physical safety in VLA models. We systematically uncover unsafe behaviors through a two-stage process: (I) \textbf{Risk Scenario Synthesis} constructs a valid and task-feasible initial risk scene. Specifically, it identifies critical interaction regions from benign trajectories and positions the risk factor within these regions, aiming to entangle it with the VLA's execution flow and elicit a target unsafe behavior. (II) \textbf{Risk Amplification} ensures stable elicitation across heterogeneous models. It iteratively refines the risk factor state through gradient-free optimization guided by trajectory features. Experiments on six representative VLA models show that RedVLA uncovers diverse unsafe behaviors and achieves the ASR up to 95.5\% within 10 optimization iterations. To mitigate these risks, we further propose SimpleVLA-Guard, a lightweight safety guard built from RedVLA-generated data. Our data, assets, and code are available \href{https://redvla.github.io}{here}.

</details>
